HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B012","root_001609/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0dfda61e556c402d2cc6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:9:1\",\"root_000053\",[\"B012\"]],[\"101:9:2\",\"root_001609\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Arzu ve eğilim boyutu, kişinin yöneldiği şeyin sonunda zorunlu düşüş hedefi olmasını düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_13a6b8fdd14e285defb6","text":"3. **The source noun becomes a destination vector.** `ء م م B012` covers aiming, intending, and heading toward, and `ه و ي B002` covers downward plunging. The result particle `فَـ` links light balances to `أُمُّهُ`, while the following noun specifies the direction's endpoint. The aiming branch supplies orientation and the falling branch supplies motion. I infer that `أُمُّهُ` can retain a dormant destination image alongside its overt noun sense; this is not a grammatical reparse. The ayah consequently presents the abyss as what the person is now headed toward as well as what receives him.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B001","root_000053/B002","root_000053/B003","root_000053/B012","root_000473/B002","root_001219/B001","root_001219/B008","root_001609/B001","root_001609/B002","root_001609/B004","root_001609/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2ebb1e0f9bb2ba05a330","text":"1. Mother-source reversed into abyss. Evidence: ء م م B001, mother as bearer/nurturer; ء م م B002, origin/gathering point/reference; ه و ي B002, falling into an abyss. Structural cue: the light scales of 101:8 are followed by فَأُمُّهُ. Mechanism: the place one returns to as source becomes the place into which one falls. Abductive move: I supply reversal: origin and refuge are inverted into destination and destruction. Reading change: \"his mother\" reads as a horrifying homecoming, not only a euphemism for outcome. 2. Void as parent. Evidence: ه و ي B001, air/void/hollowness; ء م م B002, gathering point; ق ر ع B008/B011 earlier, stripped bare surface; خ ف ف B001, lightness. Structural cue: weightlessness leads to Hawiyah, and the surah has stripped people and mountains of stable mass. Mechanism: the underweighted person belongs to hollow space; emptiness becomes the gathering center. Abductive move: I infer ontological affinity between lightness, strippedness, and void. Reading change: هاوية is not only a pit but the hollow parent of a hollowed self. 3. The wound-mouth of the struck head. Evidence: ء م م B003, head-core and wound reaching it; ه و ي B007, gaping wound or hollowed body; ق ر ع B001, striking. Structural cue: the surah begins with striking and later says \"mother/head-core\" near a gaping Hawiyah. Mechanism: the Qarīʿah's blow opens the protected core into a mouth-like abyss. Abductive move: I supply a bodily compression of event, head, wound, and pit. Reading change: أمه هاوية can be read somatically: the inner core opened by judgment. 4. Mis-aimed desire becomes destination. Evidence: ء م م B012, aiming/heading toward; ه و ي B004, desire/inclination; د ر ي B002 in 101:3/10, aiming at something; خ ف ف B004, being unsettled or misled. Structural cue: the interrogative guidance of 101:3 and 101:10 surrounds this outcome. Mechanism: what the self inclines toward becomes the path it is aimed down. Abductive move: I infer moral trajectory from desire-branch to destination-branch. Reading change: Hawiyah reads as both wanted direction and imposed fall.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B001","root_001609/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4ad411537fdaa2bcf31d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:9:1\",\"root_000053\",[\"B001\"]],[\"101:9:2\",\"root_001609\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Analık ve besleyicilik, uçurumla birleşince güvenli kökeni yıkıcı bir kabul yerine çeviren tersine dönmüş bir eve dönüş oluşturur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B012","root_001609/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_685fa6d4ce3d7f0b4752","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:9:1\",\"root_000053\",[\"B012\"]],[\"101:9:2\",\"root_001609\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yönelme boyutu, uçurumu yalnız düşülen yer değil kişinin hükümle sevk edildiği hedef haline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001609/B001","root_001609/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_734dc920cc021c4aec69","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:9:2\",\"root_001609\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Boşluk ve oyukluk boyutu, hafif bulunan kişinin hiçbir desteğin tutmadığı içi boş bir derinliğe ait oluşunu düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_83d0419970d58c8831c7","text":"2. **Dispersion ends in a hostile gathering point.** `ء م م B002` supplies origin, gathering point, and reference. People are first `مَبْثُوث` in 101:4, then individual scale clauses replace the undifferentiated crowd in 101:6-8. The fixed `أُمُّهُ` gives the light case a new center that gathers the now-individual trajectory, while `هَاوِيَةٌ` makes that center an abyss. The packet supplies dispersion, individuation, and destination; their regathering relation is abductive. The line therefore reads not as continued chaos but as terrible re-centering.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001609/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_91976a8ae2136ccde5fa","text":"4. **A fall into evacuated support.** `ه و ي B001` supplies air, void, and hollowness, while `ه و ي B002` supplies the abyss and fall. Earlier, people lose collective orientation, mountains lose material cohesion, and the fixed person's balances lose weight. Those successive removals activate void as the medium and condition of the fall: there is no stable support left to arrest it. The packet gives each loss separately; their cumulative support-removal is my abductive mechanism. `هَاوِيَةٌ` thus reads as both depth and a state emptied of sustaining structure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001609/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9def2890b2e375156107","text":"101:11 was decisive: Hawiyah is not left as a bare abyss but becomes fire, heat, enclosure, and black depth. That later gloss made the mother-source reversal more severe and also activated ه و ي B001/B007 as hollow opening rather than only falling.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B001","root_000358/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ad13f11c7ea78706ead0","text":"The pronoun question in 101:10 delayed resolution and preserved the collision between mother and abyss rather than immediately smoothing it. The answer `نَارٌ حَامِيَةٌ` in 101:11 then transformed `ء م م B001` most sharply: the receiving enclosure is thermal and anti-nurturing. Its `ح م ي B002` protection/prevention branch also makes the destination an exclusionary preserve, strengthening `ء م م B002` as hostile re-centering.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B003","root_001609/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_db6ff632c77ec2ff00de","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:9:1\",\"root_000053\",[\"B003\"]],[\"101:9:2\",\"root_001609\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Başın merkezine ulaşan yara ile oyulmuş beden çağrışımları, uçurumu darbenin iç çekirdekte açtığı yara ağzı gibi tasarlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B002","root_001609/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e6dcdb69a62e46440196","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:9:1\",\"root_000053\",[\"B002\"]],[\"101:9:2\",\"root_001609\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ana sözcüğünün köken ve toplanma merkezi boyutu, önce dağılan kişiyi şimdi düşmanca bir merkezde yeniden toplar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fb71e19d7cc77fd55d9c","text":"1. **A maternal enclosure with nurture reversed.** `ء م م B001` supplies mother as bearer and nurturer, while `ه و ي B002` supplies falling into an abyss. The possessive predicate joins the intimate source of nurture to a destructive depth, and 101:11 identifies the latter through `نَارٌ حَامِيَةٌ`. The mother branch gives the abyss the role of receiving enclosure, but its expected care is reversed into heat. I infer enclosure from the maternal relation; the text does not describe spatial walls. The ayah reads as an anti-nurturing homecoming, preserving rather than resolving the shock between its two nouns.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
